# RE-06: Encounter Tables, Enemy Records & Battle Drops

Generated by `decomp/tools/encounters.py` from US Red Sun (`bn4_rs_us.gba`) and US Blue Moon (`bn4_bm_us.gba`).
Status: **V** = verified in disassembly and both US ROMs.

## 1. Summary

- **Enemy records (`BN4_ENEMY_STATS`, `0x08011888`):** **254** 12-byte records (viruses `0x01`–`0x7F`, Navis/bosses `0x80`–`0xFC`), **100% identical** between US Red Sun and US Blue Moon.
- **Random encounter tables (`BN4_BATTLE_TABLES`, `0x08018014`):** **512** non-empty cyberworld map encounter slots across groups `0x80`–`0x94` (including base `+0x00`, wandering Ghost Navi `+0x10`, and story invasion `+0x20`/`+0x30` tables), **0 differences** between US Red Sun and US Blue Moon.
- **Story/tournament/boss battles (`BN4_STORY_BATTLES`, RS `0x080FA900` / BM `0x080FA90C`):** **245** 12-byte battle records, **0 differences** between US Red Sun and US Blue Moon.
- **Battle drop tables (`BN4_REWARD_ROWS`, RS `0x080F5F50` / BM `0x080F5F5C`):** **276** rows × 20 `u16` rewards (`0x00`–`0xFD` per enemy + `0x100`–`0x113` for the 20 Dark Soul Navi chip drops). **Exactly 64 virus rows differ** between Red Sun and Blue Moon — exclusively in **chip codes** (e.g., Mettaur `Guard1 L` in RS vs `Guard1 V` in BM; VolGear swaps `FlmLine1 F`/`H`; Billy `Thunder1 L` vs `P`).
- **Playthrough tier scaling (`BN4_ID_RAISE`, `0x0800B036`):** BN4 does **not** store separate Normal/Hard/Super Hard encounter tables. Instead, every spawned enemy's ID is raised dynamically by `tier = min(GetPlaythrough(), 2)` unless bit 7 (`0x80`) of battle record `+6` is set: **viruses (`< 0x80`)** increase by `+2 * tier` (V1→V2→V3, V1EX→V2EX→V3EX, clamped to version 5), and **Navis (`0x80`–`0xE3`)** increase by `+1 * tier` (V1→α→β, clamped to version 2).

## 2. Code Routines (V)

| RS Address | BM Address | Segment | Name | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `0x08007BEC` | `0x08007BEC` | `code_00` | `BN4_STORY_BATTLE` | r0 battle_id: returns &BN4_STORY_BATTLES[r0] (12-byte record) |
| `0x08007DC0` | `0x08007DC0` | `code_00` | `BattleGetBackgroundOrMode` | Reads battle record +2; adds 0x3C * min(playthrough, 2) unless bit 7 of +6 set |
| `0x0800B036` | `0x0800B036` | `code_00` | `BN4_ID_RAISE` | Raises enemy id by playthrough tier: virus +2*tier (cap ver 5), Navi +1*tier (cap ver 2), unless bit 7 of battle+6 set |
| `0x08011878` | `0x08011878` | `code_01` | `BN4_ENEMY_RECORD` | r0 enemy_id: returns &BN4_ENEMY_STATS[r0] (12-byte record) |
| `0x08021A98` | `0x08021A9C` | `code_02` | `RewardDecode` | Decodes 16-bit reward word: kind 0=chip(id|code<<9), 1=Zenny, 2=HP, 3=BugFrags, 0xFFFF=None |
| `0x08050228` | `0x08050234` | `code_04` | `ScriptCmd_startRandomBattle` | Script command FB 02: calls BN4_ROLL(mask=0x1F) and enters battle |
| `0x080F53D8` | `0x080F53E4` | `code_09` | `Overworld_TryRandomEncounter` | Step-counter encounter trigger: calls BN4_ROLL with NaviCust element mask (fallback 0x1F) |
| `0x080F5448` | `0x080F5454` | `code_09` | `BN4_ROLL` | Rolls weighted random encounter for current (group, map + BN4_STORY_OFFSET) filtered by r2 mask |
| `0x080F54B8` | `0x080F54C4` | `code_09` | `BN4_STORY_OFFSET` | Per-group map-slot offset: +0x10 for wandering Ghost Navi, +0x20/+0x30 for story invasions |
| `0x080F580C` | `0x080F5818` | `code_09` | `BN4_REWARD_PICK` | Selects post-battle reward from BN4_REWARD_ROWS (handles normal, Collect, and Dark Soul drops) |
| `0x080F5998` | `0x080F59A4` | `code_09` | `RewardPickBustingOffset` | Computes byte offset in 40-byte reward row from Busting Level (BN4_BUSTING_TABLE), RNG, and HP <= 3/8 |
| `0x080F5EC4` | `0x080F5ED0` | `code_09` | `BN4_REWARD_ROW_OF` | Maps enemy id -> reward row (redirects to 0x100..0x113 Dark Soul rows under Dark Karma) |
| `0x080F5F40` | `0x080F5F4C` | `code_09` | `RewardGetRowPtr` | r0 row: returns &BN4_REWARD_ROWS[row] (row * 0x28) |

## 3. Table Layouts (V)

| Table | RS Address | BM Address | Size / Layout |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BN4_ENEMY_STATS` | `0x08011888` | `0x08011888` | 254 × 12 bytes: `{u16 hp_elem (bits 0..11 HP, bits 12..15 elem), u8 t1_type, u8 cls (0x04 virus / 0x08 Navi), u8 family, u8 version (0..5 / 0..3), s8 unk6, u8 unk7, u8 unk8, u8 unk9, u8 base_atk, u8 unk11}` |
| `BN4_BATTLE_TABLES` | `0x08017FB8` | `0x08017FB8` | 2 pointers `{rw_groups=0x08017FC0, cw_groups=0x08018014}`; `cw_groups` holds 21 group pointers (`0x80`–`0x94`) → per-map pointer arrays → `0xFF`-terminated 12-byte battle records |
| `BN4_BUSTING_TABLE` | `0x0801881D` | `0x0801881D` | 12 × 16 bytes (Busting Level 0..11 × 16 RNG slots), each byte is `col * 4` (`0, 4, 8, 12, 16`) |
| `BN4_REWARD_ROWS` | `0x080F5F50` | `0x080F5F5C` | 276 × 40 bytes (20 `u16`: `0..9` when `HP > 3/8 MaxHP`, `10..19` when `HP <= 3/8 MaxHP`; 5 Busting columns × 2 50/50 slots each) |
| `BN4_STORY_BATTLES` | `0x080FA900` | `0x080FA90C` | 245 × 12-byte battle records: `{u8 weight, u8 b1, u8 bg_or_mode, u8 b3, u8 b4, u8 b5, u8 flags, u8 b7, ptr formation}` |
| Formation list | `0x080FB47C`… | `+0xC` | `0xFF`-terminated 4-byte records: `{u8 id, u8 col (1..6), u8 row (1..3), u8 kind (0=MegaMan, 1=Enemy, 2+=Obstacle)}` |

### Busting Level → Drop Column Odds (`BN4_BUSTING_TABLE`, `0x0801881D`)

Within the chosen column `C0`–`C4`, `RewardPickBustingOffset` picks slot `2*C` or `2*C+1` with 50/50 odds (`(rng >> 8) & 1`). Low HP (`HP <= (MaxHP >> 3) * 3`) uses the second 10-slot half-row (`+0x14`).

| Busting Level | Col 0 | Col 1 | Col 2 | Col 3 | Col 4 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 16/16 (100.00%) | — | — | — | — |
| 1 | 16/16 (100.00%) | — | — | — | — |
| 2 | 16/16 (100.00%) | — | — | — | — |
| 3 | 16/16 (100.00%) | — | — | — | — |
| 4 | 8/16 (50.00%) | 8/16 (50.00%) | — | — | — |
| 5 | — | 8/16 (50.00%) | 8/16 (50.00%) | — | — |
| 6 | — | — | 16/16 (100.00%) | — | — |
| 7 | — | — | 8/16 (50.00%) | 8/16 (50.00%) | — |
| 8 | — | — | — | 16/16 (100.00%) | — |
| 9 | — | — | — | 15/16 (93.75%) | 1/16 (6.25%) |
| 10 | — | — | — | 8/16 (50.00%) | 8/16 (50.00%) |
| S (11) | — | — | — | — | 16/16 (100.00%) |

## 4. Cyberworld Random Encounter Tables (`BN4_BATTLE_TABLES`)

Each map's base encounters sit at slot `map`. For net areas (`0x90`–`0x94`), `BN4_STORY_OFFSET` adds `+0x10` once the area's V2 Ghost Navi flag is set (adding the wandering Ω Ghost Navi to the encounter pool), `+0x20` when story flag `0x1C9`/`0x1CA` is set, and `+0x30` when story flag `0x1CB` is set. Unused map slots within a 16-entry band point to a shared default/dummy table and are collapsed below. Remember `BN4_ID_RAISE` upgrades non-fixed enemies (`+2*tier` viruses, `+1*tier` Navis) on Hard/Super Hard.

## 5. Every Enemy and Navi Record (`BN4_ENEMY_STATS` + `BN4_REWARD_ROWS`)

Drops are shown as `C0 | C1 | C2 | C3 | C4` (5 Busting columns; `A / B` = 50/50 pair within the column) for **High HP (`> 3/8`)** (RS). Rows marked `★` differ in Blue Moon (see §7 for every RS vs BM difference). Low-HP (`<= 3/8`) rows are identical to High-HP except where noted (typically replacing low-busting Zenny with `HP+` recovery or shifting a chip slot).

### Dark Soul Navi Drop Rows (`0x100`–`0x113` in `BN4_REWARD_ROWS`)

Returned by `BN4_REWARD_ROW_OF` (`0x080F5EC4`) when MegaMan's Dark Karma exceeds the threshold (`sub_0800F5A6() > 0x1D5`). All 20 rows are identical in RS and BM.

## 7. Red Sun vs Blue Moon Differences (64 Virus Drop Rows)

- `BN4_ENEMY_STATS` (`0x08011888`): **0 differences**.
- `BN4_BUSTING_TABLE` (`0x0801881D`): **0 differences**.
- `BN4_STORY_BATTLES` (`0x080FA900` RS / `0x080FA90C` BM, 245 battles): **0 differences**.
- `BN4_BATTLE_TABLES` (all 21 cyberworld groups): **0 differences**.
- `BN4_REWARD_ROWS` (`0x080F5F50` RS / `0x080F5F5C` BM): **64 virus rows differ**, listed in full below (each entry shows High-HP slots `0..9` that differ; Low-HP slots `10..19` mirror the same chip-code changes).

## 8. Proposed Symbol Names (`names.txt` format)

```
08007DC0 08007DC0 code   GEMINI       BattleGetBackgroundOrMode        Reads battle record +2; adds 0x3C * min(playthrough, 2) unless bit 7 of +6 set
08021A98 08021A9C code   GEMINI       RewardDecode                     Decodes 16-bit reward word: kind 0=chip, 1=Zenny, 2=HP, 3=BugFrags, 0xFFFF=None
080F53D8 080F53E4 code   GEMINI       Overworld_TryRandomEncounter     Step-counter encounter trigger; calls BN4_ROLL with NaviCust element mask (fallback 0x1F)
080F5998 080F59A4 code   GEMINI       RewardPickBustingOffset          Computes byte offset in 40-byte reward row from Busting Level, RNG, and HP <= 3/8
080F5F40 080F5F4C code   GEMINI       RewardGetRowPtr                  r0 row: returns &BN4_REWARD_ROWS[row] (row * 0x28)
```
